Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any chemical_engineering module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 587. The projective resolution has length at most 995 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the chemical_engineering space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 587 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 995. QED.
